1. **Identify the black nodes:** The black nodes are X and N.
2. **Find the immediate neighbors of X:**
   - X is connected to G, K, and F.
3. **Expand from neighbors of X:**
   - G is connected to H, B, and E.
   - K is connected to F.
   - F is connected to E and L.
4. **Continue expanding from the next set of neighbors:**
   - H is connected to B and C.
   - B is connected to C, I, and E.
   - E is connected to I, P, and J.
   - F is connected to L.
   - L is connected to E and M.
   - E is connected to I, P, and J.
5. **Find the path to N:**
   - Notice that B is connected to H, C, I, and E.
   - C is connected to B, H, and N.
   - N is connected to C, O, and Y.

By tracing the connections, we find the following possible paths:
- X -> G -> H -> B -> C -> N (Path length 4)
- Alternatively, checking other paths might show:
- X -> G -> B -> C -> N (Path length 3)

The shortest path is through X -> G -> B -> C -> N.

**Answer:**

Length: 3, Path: [X, G, B, C, N]
